([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/multilingual-transfer-eval) · ★3)
- **multilingual-translation**: 多语种翻译技能负责将视频中提取的文本内容（ASR转写文本和OCR识别文本）翻译为目标语种，是多语种视频分发流程的核心基础能力。该技能不仅执行语言层面的转换，还需处理语种混合识别、时间轴保持、情感标注传递等视频翻译特有的技术挑战。 ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-video/scopes/multilingual-video-distribution/skills/multilingual-translation) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **multilingual-translation**: 維護命理網站的多語閱讀、文字互譯與字幕文字翻譯；適用語言切換和翻譯卡片，不更改命盤運算或易經老師的服務設定。 ([sam31094121/fortune_telling](https://github.com/sam31094121/fortune_telling/tree/HEAD/docs/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E6%88%B0%E9%AC%A5%E6%AA%94%E6%A1%88/%E7%BF%BB%E8%AD%AF/multilingual-translation))
- **multilingual-translation-eval**: Evaluates large language models' multilingual instruction-following and non-English-centric translation capabilities across diverse language pairs and prompting strategies. The protocol tests how model performance varies when prompts are provided in different languages (e.g., Chinese, Finnish, English) versus automatically translated prompts. Use when the user wants to benchmark on NTREX-128, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports ChrF. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/multilingual-translation-eval) · ★3)
